# open_sol templates

Regenerate with: python -m open_sol describe --update-reference (writes this file and api_reference.md)

Generated by describe() from the templates (the body below is describe('templates')); selftest fails while this file differs from it. Types, settings and help texts are in api_reference.md, whose Templates section lists every template with the physics, feature, coupling, solver and study types it uses.

## Templates

Worked examples: every template validates without errors (selftest templates_validate), and modifying the closest one is the most reliable way to write a valid model. api.template(key) returns one as a ModelSpec, python -m open_sol template KEY FILE writes its model file.

### `dielectric_cylinder` - Dielectric cylinder

EM: a 550 nm gaussian point source scatters off a glass cylinder; field probe and transmitted flux spectrum.

```json
{
  "title": "Dielectric cylinder",
  "description": "",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "lambda0", "expression": "550*nm", "description": "source centre wavelength"},
    {"name": "r_cylinder", "expression": "0.6*um", "description": "cylinder radius"}
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 4e-06,
    "size_y": 4e-06,
    "size_z": null,
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"
  },
  "geometry": [
    {"name": "cylinder", "kind": "circle", "params": {"cx": 2e-06, "cy": 2e-06, "r": "r_cylinder"}},
    {"name": "source_point", "kind": "point", "params": {"x": 8e-07, "y": 2e-06}},
    {"name": "probe_point", "kind": "point", "params": {"x": 3e-06, "y": 2e-06}},
    {"name": "flux_line", "kind": "line", "params": {"x0": 3.4e-06, "y0": 5e-07, "x1": 3.4e-06, "y1": 3.5e-06}}
  ],
  "selections": [],
  "materials": [
    {"name": "glass", "library": null, "properties": {"eps_r": 2.25}, "dispersion": null, "selection": ["cylinder"]}
  ],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "em",
      "type": "em_fdtd_ez",
      "settings": {},
      "features": [
        {
          "name": "source",
          "type": "point_source",
          "selection": ["source_point"],
          "params": {"waveform": "gaussian", "wavelength": "lambda0", "bandwidth": 0.5, "current": 0.001},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "probe",
          "type": "field_probe",
          "selection": ["probe_point"],
          "params": {"component": "ez"},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "transmitted_flux",
          "type": "flux_monitor",
          "selection": ["flux_line"],
          "params": {"wavelength_start": 4e-07, "wavelength_stop": 8e-07, "n_wavelengths": 41},
          "enabled": true
        }
      ],
      "every": 1,
      "solvers": {},
      "mesh": ""
    }
  ],
  "couplings": [],
  "meshes": [
    {
      "name": "mesh",
      "kind": "tensor",
      "mode": "uniform",
      "dx": 2e-08,
      "max_ratio": null,
      "wavelength_min": null,
      "points_per_wavelength": null,
      "refinements": [],
      "outer_dx": null,
      "outer_ratio": null,
      "axes": [],
      "h_min": null,
      "curvature_factor": 0.3,
      "mesher": "builtin",
      "sweep_axis": "none",
      "pml_layers": 8,
      "mirror_x": false,
      "mirror_y": false,
      "size_points": [],
      "face_sizes": []
    }
  ],
  "solvers": [],
  "solver_defaults": {
    "backend": "auto",
    "precision": "auto",
    "subpixel_smoothing": false,
    "conformal_boundaries": false,
    "memory_check": "error"
  },
  "study": {
    "type": "time_dependent",
    "settings": {"duration": 1.5e-13, "output_interval": 2.5e-15},
    "stored_fields": [],
    "sweep": null,
    "stored_cuts": [],
    "adaptation": null,
    "estimation": null,
    "solved_physics": []
  },
  "derived_values": [],
  "analytic_curves": [],
  "derived_curves": []
}
```

### `gold_nanodisk` - Gold nanodisk

EM: TFSF plane wave on a Drude gold disk (uniform 2 nm mesh); near-field DFT and far-field pattern, 400-900 nm.

```json
{
  "title": "Gold nanodisk scattering",
  "description": "",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [{"name": "r_disk", "expression": "50*nm", "description": "disk radius"}],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 1e-06,
    "size_y": 1e-06,
    "size_z": null,
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"
  },
  "geometry": [
    {"name": "disk", "kind": "circle", "params": {"cx": 5e-07, "cy": 5e-07, "r": "r_disk"}},
    {"name": "tfsf_box", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 3.5e-07, "y0": 3.5e-07, "x1": 6.5e-07, "y1": 6.5e-07}},
    {"name": "near_field_box", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 4e-07, "y0": 4e-07, "x1": 6e-07, "y1": 6e-07}},
    {
      "name": "far_field_box",
      "kind": "rectangle",
      "params": {"x0": 2.5e-07, "y0": 2.5e-07, "x1": 7.5e-07, "y1": 7.5e-07}
    }
  ],
  "selections": [],
  "materials": [{"name": "gold", "library": "gold", "properties": {}, "dispersion": null, "selection": ["disk"]}],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "em",
      "type": "em_fdtd_ez",
      "settings": {},
      "features": [
        {
          "name": "plane_wave",
          "type": "plane_wave_tfsf",
          "selection": ["tfsf_box"],
          "params": {"waveform": "gaussian", "wavelength": 5.5e-07, "bandwidth": 0.8, "amplitude": 1.0},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "near_field",
          "type": "dft_monitor",
          "selection": ["near_field_box"],
          "params": {"wavelength_start": 4e-07, "wavelength_stop": 9e-07, "n_wavelengths": 11},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "far_field",
          "type": "far_field",
          "selection": ["far_field_box"],
          "params": {"wavelength_start": 4e-07, "wavelength_stop": 9e-07, "n_wavelengths": 11, "n_angles": 360},
          "enabled": true
        }
      ],
      "every": 1,
      "solvers": {},
      "mesh": ""
    }
  ],
  "couplings": [],
  "meshes": [
    {
      "name": "mesh",
      "kind": "tensor",
      "mode": "uniform",
      "dx": 2e-09,
      "max_ratio": null,
      "wavelength_min": null,
      "points_per_wavelength": null,
      "refinements": [],
      "outer_dx": null,
      "outer_ratio": null,
      "axes": [],
      "h_min": null,
      "curvature_factor": 0.3,
      "mesher": "builtin",
      "sweep_axis": "none",
      "pml_layers": 8,
      "mirror_x": false,
      "mirror_y": false,
      "size_points": [],
      "face_sizes": []
    }
  ],
  "solvers": [],
  "solver_defaults": {
    "backend": "auto",
    "precision": "auto",
    "subpixel_smoothing": false,
    "conformal_boundaries": false,
    "memory_check": "error"
  },
  "study": {
    "type": "time_dependent",
    "settings": {"duration": 6e-14},
    "stored_fields": [],
    "sweep": null,
    "stored_cuts": [],
    "adaptation": null,
    "estimation": null,
    "solved_physics": []
  },
  "derived_values": [],
  "analytic_curves": [],
  "derived_curves": []
}
```

### `joule_heating` - Joule heating

EM + heat: a CW gaussian beam heats a gold disk in silica; Joule and thermo-optic couplings, 293.15 K walls.

```json
{
  "title": "Joule heating of a gold disk",
  "description": "",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "E0", "expression": 1000000.0, "description": "beam peak field at the waist [V/m]"},
    {"name": "r_disk", "expression": "100*nm", "description": "disk radius"}
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 2e-06,
    "size_y": 2e-06,
    "size_z": null,
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"
  },
  "geometry": [
    {"name": "disk", "kind": "circle", "params": {"cx": 1e-06, "cy": 1e-06, "r": "r_disk"}},
    {"name": "beam_line", "kind": "line", "params": {"x0": 2e-07, "y0": 4e-07, "x1": 1.8e-06, "y1": 4e-07}},
    {"name": "disk_center", "kind": "point", "params": {"x": 1e-06, "y": 1e-06}}
  ],
  "selections": [],
  "materials": [
    {"name": "substrate", "library": "silica", "properties": {}, "dispersion": null, "selection": ["all"]},
    {"name": "gold", "library": "gold", "properties": {}, "dispersion": null, "selection": ["disk"]}
  ],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "em",
      "type": "em_fdtd_ez",
      "settings": {"track_absorption": true},
      "features": [
        {
          "name": "beam",
          "type": "gaussian_beam",
          "selection": ["beam_line"],
          "params": {"waveform": "cw", "wavelength": 5.32e-07, "amplitude": "E0", "waist": 4e-07},
          "enabled": true
        }
      ],
      "every": 1,
      "solvers": {},
      "mesh": ""
    },
    {
      "name": "heat",
      "type": "heat",
      "settings": {},
      "features": [
        {
          "name": "walls",
          "type": "temperature",
          "selection": ["x_lo", "x_hi", "y_lo", "y_hi"],
          "params": {"temperature": 293.15},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "disk_temperature",
          "type": "temperature_probe",
          "selection": ["disk_center"],
          "params": {},
          "enabled": true
        }
      ],
      "every": 500,
      "solvers": {},
      "mesh": ""
    }
  ],
  "couplings": [
    {
      "name": "joule",
      "type": "em_heat_joule",
      "source": "em",
      "target": "heat",
      "every": null,
      "params": {},
      "enabled": true
    },
    {
      "name": "thermo_optic",
      "type": "heat_em_thermo_optic",
      "source": "heat",
      "target": "em",
      "every": null,
      "params": {},
      "enabled": true
    }
  ],
  "meshes": [
    {
      "name": "mesh",
      "kind": "tensor",
      "mode": "uniform",
      "dx": 1e-08,
      "max_ratio": null,
      "wavelength_min": null,
      "points_per_wavelength": null,
      "refinements": [],
      "outer_dx": null,
      "outer_ratio": null,
      "axes": [],
      "h_min": null,
      "curvature_factor": 0.3,
      "mesher": "builtin",
      "sweep_axis": "none",
      "pml_layers": 8,
      "mirror_x": false,
      "mirror_y": false,
      "size_points": [],
      "face_sizes": []
    }
  ],
  "solvers": [],
  "solver_defaults": {
    "backend": "auto",
    "precision": "auto",
    "subpixel_smoothing": false,
    "conformal_boundaries": false,
    "memory_check": "error"
  },
  "study": {
    "type": "time_dependent",
    "settings": {"duration": 3e-13},
    "stored_fields": [],
    "sweep": null,
    "stored_cuts": [],

  "derived_values": [
    {
```

### `transient_heat_sink` - Transient heat sink

Heat (transient): a heater in a silicon block, convection on top, fixed temperature at the bottom.

```json
{
  "title": "Transient heat sink",
  "description": "",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "Q0", "expression": 50000000000.0, "description": "heater power density [W/m^3]"},
    {"name": "h_top", "expression": 10000.0, "description": "top-face heat transfer coefficient [W/(m^2 K)]"}
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 0.002,
    "size_y": 0.001,
    "size_z": null,
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"
  },
  "geometry": [
    {"name": "heater", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 0.0008, "y0": 0.00084, "x1": 0.0012, "y1": 0.00094}},
    {"name": "probe_point", "kind": "point", "params": {"x": 0.001, "y": 0.0009}}
  ],
  "selections": [],
  "materials": [{"name": "silicon", "library": "silicon", "properties": {}, "dispersion": null, "selection": ["all"]}],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "heat",
      "type": "heat",
      "settings": {"time_mode": "transient", "time_step": 0.0001},
      "features": [
        {"name": "heat_source", "type": "heat_source", "selection": ["heater"], "params": {"Q": "Q0"}, "enabled": true},
        {
          "name": "top_cooling",
          "type": "convection",
          "selection": ["y_hi"],
          "params": {"h": "h_top", "T_ext": 293.15},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "heat_sink",
          "type": "temperature",
          "selection": ["y_lo"],
          "params": {"temperature": 293.15},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "heater_temperature",
          "type": "temperature_probe",
          "selection": ["probe_point"],
          "params": {},
          "enabled": true
        }
      ],
      "every": 1,
      "solvers": {},
      "mesh": ""
    }
  ],
  "couplings": [],
  "meshes": [
    {
      "name": "mesh",
      "kind": "tensor",
      "mode": "uniform",
      "dx": 2e-05,
      "max_ratio": null,
      "wavelength_min": null,
      "points_per_wavelength": null,

      "outer_ratio": null,
      "axes": [],
      "h_min": null,
      "curvature_factor": 0.3,
      "mesher": "builtin",
      "sweep_axis": "none",
      "pml_layers": 8,
      "mirror_x": false,
      "mirror_y": false,
      "size_points": [],
      "face_sizes": []
    }
  ],
  "solvers": [],
  "solver_defaults": {
    "backend": "auto",
    "precision": "auto",
    "subpixel_smoothing": false,
    "conformal_boundaries": false,
    "memory_check": "error"
  },
  "study": {
```

### `nonlinear_conduction` - Nonlinear conduction

Heat (stationary): temperature-dependent conductivity k_300*(300/T)**1.3 around a heated core.

```json
{
  "title": "Nonlinear heat conduction",
  "description": "",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "k_300", "expression": 130.0, "description": "thermal conductivity at 300 K [W/(m K)]"},
    {"name": "Q0", "expression": 1000000000000.0, "description": "core power density [W/m^3]"}
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 0.001,
    "size_y": 0.001,
    "size_z": null,
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"
  },
  "geometry": [{"name": "core", "kind": "circle", "params": {"cx": 0.0005, "cy": 0.0005, "r": 0.0001}}],
  "selections": [],
  "materials": [
    {
      "name": "silicon",
      "library": "silicon",
      "properties": {"k": "k_300*(300/T)**1.3"},
      "dispersion": null,
      "selection": ["all"]
    }
  ],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "heat",
      "type": "heat",
      "settings": {"T0": 300.0},
      "features": [
        {"name": "heater", "type": "heat_source", "selection": ["core"], "params": {"Q": "Q0"}, "enabled": true},
        {
          "name": "walls",
          "type": "temperature",
```

### `photoacoustic_disc` - Photoacoustic disc

elastic_waves (time_dependent): a 1 mm absorbing disc in water heated by a 100 ns pulse (heat_pulse_source, Grueneisen 0.2): the thermoelastic pressure (initial p0 = Grueneisen x absorbed energy density, ~35 kPa) leaves the disc as an N-shaped pulse recorded by a pressure probe 2 mm away.

### `rigid_disk_scattering` - Rigid disk scattering (pressure acoustics)

Axisymmetric pressure_acoustics: a thin rigid disk in a plane wave (scattered-field background, PMLs), the mean pressure on its top and bottom faces from k a = 0.05 to 0.3 (log sweep); the low-frequency pressure difference is (8 / 3 pi) k a p0.

### `fresnel_interface` - Fresnel interface

EM: a 1 um gaussian pulse crosses a vacuum / glass (eps_r = 4) interface in a y-periodic strip; flux spectrum 0.9-1.1 um behind it (8/9 of a vacuum run).

```json

  "title": "Fresnel transmission at a dielectric interface",
```

### `gold_nanodisk_hz` - Gold nanodisk (in-plane electric field)

EM (H_z polarisation): TFSF plane wave with in-plane E on a Drude gold disk (uniform 2 nm mesh, 200 fs); near-field DFT of |E|^2 shows the particle plasmon, plus the far-field pattern, 400-900 nm.

### `directional_coupler` - Directional coupler (in-plane electric field)

EM (H_z polarisation): two 0.3 um silicon strips in silica with a 4 um long, 0.2 um gap coupling section and cosine S-bends (uniform 20 nm mesh); a 1.55 um gaussian mode source in strip A and mode monitors give the through, cross and reflected power fractions (|S|^2) and phases, 1.45-1.65 um.

### `fd_joule_heating` - Joule heating of a gold nanowire (frequency domain)

EM (FDFD, E_z) + heat, stationary study: a 530 nm gaussian beam heats a 30 nm gold nanowire in water by about 9 K; Joule and thermo-optic couplings, convection on the outer faces, max T and the absorbed power.

```json
{
  "title": "Joule heating of a gold nanowire (frequency domain)",
  "description": "",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "lambda0", "expression": "530*nm", "description": "vacuum wavelength of the beam"},
    {"name": "E0", "expression": 1000000.0, "description": "beam peak field at the waist [V/m]"},
    {"name": "r_wire", "expression": "30*nm", "description": "nanowire radius"}

    "size_y": 1e-06,
```

### `effective_index_rib_waveguide` - Rib waveguide (effective index 2.5D)

EM (FDFD, H_z): top view of a 0.5 um silicon rib (220 nm rib on a 90 nm slab, silica above and below) collapsed by the effective-index method (effective_index features); a 1.55 um mode source and a mode monitor give the lateral mode index and transmission.

### `disk_resonator_eigenmodes` - Disk resonator eigenmodes

EM FDFD (E_z), eigenfrequency study: the whispering-gallery resonances of a silicon disk (eps_r 12.11, radius r_disk = 1 um) in air nearest 1.55 um (6 resonances; each mode is a near-degenerate pair on the staircase disk), with wavelength, Q and field shape per resonance; derived lambda_1 and Q_1 of the nearest one. The same modes as the FDTD disk_resonator template (ring-down), without a source or a long run.

### `cylinder_harmonic_fdtd` - Dielectric cylinder, time-domain spectrum

EM FDTD (E_z) in a frequency_domain study (harmonic FDTD): one plane-wave pulse (TFSF box, 1 um, bandwidth 0.5) scatters off a dielectric cylinder (eps_r 4, radius r_cyl = 0.3 um) and gives the complex E_z phasor of every study wavelength 0.85-1.2 um (8 values, one snapshot each) from a single time-domain run; switch the physics to em_fdfd_ez for the same maps by one frequency-domain solve per wavelength.

### `waveguide_bend_optimization` - Waveguide bend optimization

EM FDFD (E_z) at 1.55 um, optimization study: a 0.28 um silicon strip in silica turns by 90 degrees inside a 1.2 x 1.2 um design region (density from silica to silicon, filter radius 80 nm, tanh projection reaching beta 8 by a continuation over 3 stages: beta 2, 4, 8, at most 25 L-BFGS-B iterations each). Objective: the mode 1 power leaving along +y (mode_power_positive). The design_density field is stored per iteration; the design_region result shows J and the grayness versus iteration.

```json
{
  "title": "Waveguide bend optimization (E_z, 1.55 um)",
  "description": "",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 3e-06,
    "size_y": 3e-06,
    "size_z": null,

    "angle_wrap": "none"

    {
      "name": "output_strip",
      "kind": "rectangle",
      "params": {"x0": 1.46e-06, "y0": 2.2e-06, "x1": 1.74e-06, "y1": 3e-06}
    },
    {"name": "bend", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 1e-06, "y0": 1e-06, "x1": 2.2e-06, "y1": 2.2e-06}},
    {"name": "source_line", "kind": "line", "params": {"x0": 5.1e-07, "y0": 0.0, "x1": 5.1e-07, "y1": 3e-06}},
    {"name": "output_line", "kind": "line", "params": {"x0": 0.0, "y0": 2.61e-06, "x1": 3e-06, "y1": 2.61e-06}}
  ],
  "selections": [],

    {"name": "silica", "library": null, "properties": {"eps_r": 2.085}, "dispersion": null, "selection": ["all"]},
    {
      "name": "silicon",
      "library": null,
      "properties": {"eps_r": 12.08},
      "dispersion": null,
      "selection": ["input_strip", "output_strip"]
    }
  ],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "em",
      "type": "em_fdfd_ez",
      "settings": {"wavelength": 1.55e-06},
      "features": [
        {
          "name": "source",
          "type": "mode_source",
          "selection": ["source_line"],
          "params": {"mode_number": 1, "direction": "positive", "power": 1.0, "phase": 0.0},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "transmission",

          "params": {"quantity": "mode_power_positive", "mode_number": 1, "weight": 1.0},

        },
        {
          "name": "design",

          "params": {
            "eps_min": 2.085,
            "eps_max": 12.08,
            "initial_density": 0.5,
            "filter_radius": 8e-08,
            "projection_beta": 8.0,
            "projection_eta": 0.5
          },
          "enabled": true
        }
      ],
      "every": 1,
      "solvers": {},
      "mesh": ""
    }
  ],
  "couplings": [],
  "meshes": [
    {
      "name": "mesh",
      "kind": "tensor",
      "mode": "uniform",
      "dx": 2e-08,
      "max_ratio": null,
      "wavelength_min": null,
      "points_per_wavelength": null,

      "outer_ratio": null,
```

### `bloch_band_diagram` - Photonic crystal band diagram

EM (E_z): one unit cell of a square lattice of dielectric rods (eps_r 8.9, r = 0.2 a, a = 0.5 um), Bloch-periodic with bloch_kx = k_fraction 2 pi / a swept over 0-0.5 (Gamma-X); a point-source pulse and a resonance finder per k give the band frequencies (TM gap ~1.14-1.56 um).

### `nonlinear_silicon_slab` - Nonlinear silicon slab

EM (E_z): a 1.55 um gaussian pulse of 1 GV/m peak crosses a 2 um silicon slab with Kerr (n2 6e-18 m^2/W), two-photon absorption (beta 8e-12 m/W) and TPA free carriers (lifetime 1 ns, FCA 1.45e-21 m^2, dn/dN -1.35e-27 m^3) in a y-periodic strip; transmitted flux spectrum, probe trace and the carrier_density map.

### `phase_change_gst_switch` - GST phase-change switch

EM (E_z, frequency domain at 1.55 um) + transient heat, time dependent: a cw line source (3 MV/m) heats a 100 nm GST-225 film on 0.5 um of silica over a 293 K heat sink; em_heat_joule feeds the absorbed power to heat, heat_em_phase_change crystallises the film from its temperature history (more absorbing as it crystallises), and the FDFD re-solves after every heat step; crystallinity and T maps, film temperature and crystallinity traces.

Heat + EM cross-section modes, mode_analysis with a sweep over P_heater (0, 10, 20 W/m): a gold heater 1 um above a 500 x 220 nm silicon strip in silica, substrate face at 293.15 K; steady heat, heat_em_thermo_optic, then the modes on the heated permittivity; derived n_eff_1 (dn_eff ~5e-4 at 20 W/m: ~2 rad per mm) and T_core.

### `pn_phase_shifter_depletion` - PN phase shifter (prescribed carriers)

EM cross-section modes, mode_analysis with a sweep over the reverse bias V_R (0, 2, 4 V): a 500 x 220 nm silicon rib on a 90 nm slab in silica with a lateral pn junction at its centre (N_A = N_D = 5e17 cm^-3). The carrier densities are prescribed by the depletion approximation in closed form (none inside the depletion width W(V_R), the doping outside) and enter eps_r / sigma_e of silicon through the Soref-Bennett plasma dispersion at 1.55 um; derived n_eff_1 and loss_1 (dB/cm) per bias (dn_eff ~1e-4 between 0 and 4 V).

```json
{
  "title": "PN phase shifter (prescribed carriers)",
  "description": "",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {
      "name": "N_A",
      "expression": 5e+23,
      "description": "acceptor density of the p side [m^-3] (1e24 m^-3 = 1e18 cm^-3)"
    },
    {"name": "N_D", "expression": 5e+23, "description": "donor density of the n side [m^-3]"},
    {"name": "V_R", "expression": 4.0, "description": "reverse bias [V] (> 0 widens the depletion region)"},
    {"name": "x_j", "expression": 2e-06, "description": "junction position [m] (p side below, n side above)"},
    {"name": "n_Si", "expression": 3.476, "description": "refractive index of undoped silicon at 1.55 um"},
    {"name": "eps_si", "expression": 11.7, "description": "static relative permittivity of silicon"},
    {"name": "n_i", "expression": 1e+16, "description": "intrinsic carrier density of silicon at 300 K [m^-3]"},
    {"name": "V_T", "expression": 0.025852, "description": "thermal voltage kT / q at 300 K [V]"},
    {"name": "q_e", "expression": 1.602176634e-19, "description": "elementary charge [C]"},
    {"name": "V_bi", "expression": "V_T * log(N_A * N_D / n_i**2)", "description": "built-in potential [V]"},
    {
      "name": "W_dep",
      "expression": "sqrt(2 * eps_si * eps0_const * (V_bi + V_R) / q_e * (N_A + N_D) / (N_A * N_D))",
      "description": "depletion width [m] (abrupt junction, depletion approximation)"
    },
    {"name": "x_n", "expression": "W_dep * N_A / (N_A + N_D)", "description": "depletion depth on the n side [m]"},
    {"name": "x_p", "expression": "W_dep * N_D / (N_A + N_D)", "description": "depletion depth on the p side [m]"}
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 4e-06,
    "size_y": 1.6e-06,
    "size_z": null,
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"
  },
  "geometry": [
    {"name": "slab_p", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 0.0, "y0": 6e-07, "x1": 2e-06, "y1": 6.9e-07}},
    {"name": "slab_n", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 2e-06, "y0": 6e-07, "x1": 4e-06, "y1": 6.9e-07}},
    {"name": "rib_p", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 1.75e-06, "y0": 6e-07, "x1": 2e-06, "y1": 8.2e-07}},
    {"name": "rib_n", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 2e-06, "y0": 6e-07, "x1": 2.25e-06, "y1": 8.2e-07}}
  ],
  "selections": [],
  "materials": [
    {"name": "cladding", "library": "silica", "properties": {}, "dispersion": null, "selection": ["all"]},
    {
      "name": "p_silicon",
      "library": "silicon",
      "properties": {
        "eps_r": "(n_Si - 8.5e-18 * (1e-6 * (where(x >= x_j - x_p, 0, N_A)))**0.8)**2",
        "sigma_e": "(n_Si - 8.5e-18 * (1e-6 * (where(x >= x_j - x_p, 0, N_A)))**0.8) * 100 * (6e-18 * (1e-6 * (where(x >= x_j - x_p, 0, N_A)))) * eps0_const * c_const"
      },
      "dispersion": null,
      "selection": ["slab_p", "rib_p"]
    },
    {
      "name": "n_silicon",
      "library": "silicon",
      "properties": {
        "eps_r": "(n_Si - 8.8e-22 * (1e-6 * (where(x < x_j + x_n, 0, N_D))))**2",
        "sigma_e": "(n_Si - 8.8e-22 * (1e-6 * (where(x < x_j + x_n, 0, N_D)))) * 100 * (8.5e-18 * (1e-6 * (where(x < x_j + x_n, 0, N_D)))) * eps0_const * c_const"
      },
      "dispersion": null,
      "selection": ["slab_n", "rib_n"]
    }
  ],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "modes",
      "type": "em_modes",
      "settings": {"wavelength": 1.55e-06, "n_modes": 2, "pml_sigma_factor": 0.7},
      "features": [{"name": "mode_table", "type": "mode_table", "selection": [], "params": {}, "enabled": true}],
      "every": 1,
      "solvers": {},
      "mesh": ""
    }
  ],
  "couplings": [],
```

### `pn_phase_shifter_electrostatics` - PN phase shifter (electrostatics)

Electrostatics + EM cross-section modes, mode_analysis with a sweep over V_R (0, 2, 4 V): the rib of 'pn_phase_shifter_depletion', but the electrostatics solves V of the depletion-approximation space charge between slab contacts at 0 V and V_bi + V_R, and the carriers follow V by Boltzmann statistics (n = N_D exp((V - V_bi - V_R) / V_T), p = N_A exp(-V / V_T), clamped at the doping): smooth depletion edges and the 2D field of the rib, read by eps_r / sigma_e (Soref-Bennett) as state-dependent materials; derived n_eff_1 and loss_1.

```json

    {"name": "n_Si", "expression": 3.476, "description": "refractive index of undoped silicon at 1.55 um"},
    {"name": "eps_si", "expression": 11.7, "description": "static relative permittivity of silicon"},
    {"name": "n_i", "expression": 1e+16, "description": "intrinsic carrier density of silicon at 300 K [m^-3]"},
    {"name": "V_T", "expression": 0.025852, "description": "thermal voltage kT / q at 300 K [V]"},
    {"name": "q_e", "expression": 1.602176634e-19, "description": "elementary charge [C]"},
    {"name": "V_bi", "expression": "V_T * log(N_A * N_D / n_i**2)", "description": "built-in potential [V]"},
    {
      "name": "W_dep",
      "expression": "sqrt(2 * eps_si * eps0_const * (V_bi + V_R) / q_e * (N_A + N_D) / (N_A * N_D))",
      "description": "depletion width [m] (abrupt junction, depletion approximation)"
    },
    {"name": "x_n", "expression": "W_dep * N_A / (N_A + N_D)", "description": "depletion depth on the n side [m]"},
    {"name": "x_p", "expression": "W_dep * N_D / (N_A + N_D)", "description": "depletion depth on the p side [m]"}
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 4e-06,
    "size_y": 1.6e-06,
    "size_z": null,
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,

    {"name": "rib_n", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 2e-06, "y0": 6e-07, "x1": 2.25e-06, "y1": 8.2e-07}},
    {"name": "contact_p", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 4e-07, "y0": 6e-07, "x1": 9e-07, "y1": 6.9e-07}},
    {"name": "contact_n", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 3.1e-06, "y0": 6e-07, "x1": 3.6e-06, "y1": 6.9e-07}}
  ],

    {
      "name": "p_silicon",
      "library": "silicon",
      "properties": {
        "eps_r": "(n_Si - 8.5e-18 * (1e-6 * (N_A * exp(min(-electrostatics_V, 0) / V_T)))**0.8)**2",
        "sigma_e": "(n_Si - 8.5e-18 * (1e-6 * (N_A * exp(min(-electrostatics_V, 0) / V_T)))**0.8) * 100 * (6e-18 * (1e-6 * (N_A * exp(min(-electrostatics_V, 0) / V_T)))) * eps0_const * c_const"
```

### `microdisk_bor_eigenmodes` - Microdisk whispering-gallery modes (axisymmetric)

EM body-of-revolution FDFD, eigenfrequency study: the whispering-gallery resonances of azimuthal order m = 15 of a silicon microdisk (eps_r 12.11, radius r_disk = 1.5 um, 240 nm thick) in air nearest 1.55 um, solved on the (r, z) half plane above its equatorial plane (pmc mirror on z_lo: quasi-TE modes, E in the disk plane), cpml on r_hi and z_hi, 20 nm mesh. Wavelength, Q (radiation into the PML) and field per resonance; derived lambda_1 and Q_1. A full 3D answer at 2D cost; list several orders in azimuthal_orders (e.g. 14, 15, 16) for the free spectral range.

```json
{
  "title": "Microdisk whispering-gallery modes (axisymmetric)",
  "description": "",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "r_disk", "expression": 1.5e-06, "description": "disk radius [m]"},
    {"name": "t_disk", "expression": 2.4e-07, "description": "disk thickness [m]"}
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 3e-06,
    "size_y": 1.2e-06,
    "size_z": null,
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"
  },
  "geometry": [
    {"name": "disk", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 0.0, "y0": 0.0, "x1": "r_disk", "y1": "t_disk / 2"}}
  ],
  "selections": [],
  "materials": [
    {"name": "silicon_disk", "library": null, "properties": {"eps_r": 12.11}, "dispersion": null, "selection": ["disk"]}
  ],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "em",
      "type": "em_fdfd_bor",
      "settings": {
        "wavelength": 1.55e-06,
```

### `photonic_crystal_slab_bands_3d` - Photonic crystal slab band diagram (3D)

em_fdtd_3d: one unit cell of a square lattice of air holes (a = 0.5 um, r = 0.3 a) in a 0.275 um eps 12 slab with vacuum and CPML above and below, x / y periodic with bloch_kx = k_fraction 2 pi / a swept over 0-0.5 (Gamma-X); an in-plane dipole on the mid-plane and a resonance finder per k give the TE-like band frequencies and Q (guided below the light line, leaky above it).

### `nanobeam_mirror_cell_bands_3d` - Nanobeam mirror cell band diagram (3D, frequency domain)

em_fdfd_3d: one mirror cell of a silicon nanobeam photonic crystal in D2O (igor_experiment 4.3: period a = 394.3 nm, hole d = 279.3 nm along y through a 595.2 x 220 nm beam, n 3.47 / 1.32), quarter cross-section (x_lo antisymmetric = PEC plane, y_lo symmetric = PMC plane: the TE-like sector), 300 nm D2O and 8 CPML cells on x_hi / y_hi, z periodic with bloch_kz = k_fraction 2 pi / a swept over 0.4-0.5 (guided, below the D2O light line); an eigenfrequency study per k lists the 4 bands nearest 1.496 um (resonance_table). At k_fraction 0.5 the dielectric (longer wavelength) and air band edges bound the TE-like gap that should contain 1.496 um; set a and d to a taper cell to follow the band-edge grading. a / 16 cells (24.6 nm) with subpixel smoothing, ~4e4 unknowns per k (one host factor each, minutes); more padding mainly raises the guided-band Q (the evanescent tail reaches the CPML).

```json
{
  "title": "Nanobeam mirror cell band diagram (3D, frequency domain)",
  "description": "em_fdfd_3d: one mirror cell of a silicon nanobeam photonic crystal in D2O (igor_experiment 4.3: period a = 394.3 nm, hole d = 279.3 nm along y through a 595.2 x 220 nm beam, n 3.47 / 1.32), quarter cross-section (x_lo antisymmetric = PEC plane, y_lo symmetric = PMC plane: the TE-like sector), 300 nm D2O and 8 CPML cells on x_hi / y_hi, z periodic with bloch_kz = k_fraction 2 pi / a swept over 0.4-0.5 (guided, below the D2O light line); an eigenfrequency study per k lists the 4 bands nearest 1.496 um (resonance_table). At k_fraction 0.5 the dielectric (longer wavelength) and air band edges bound the TE-like gap that should contain 1.496 um; set a and d to a taper cell to follow the band-edge grading. a / 16 cells (24.6 nm) with subpixel smoothing, ~4e4 unknowns per k (one host factor each, minutes); more padding mainly raises the guided-band Q (the evanescent tail reaches the CPML).",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "a", "expression": "394.3*nm", "description": "period (mirror cell; a taper cell: its a_i)"},
    {"name": "d", "expression": "279.3*nm", "description": "hole diameter (a taper cell: its d_i)"},
    {"name": "w", "expression": "595.2*nm", "description": "beam width (x)"},
    {"name": "thickness", "expression": "220*nm", "description": "beam thickness (y)"},
    {"name": "pad", "expression": "300*nm", "description": "D2O between the beam and the CPML (x and y)"},
    {"name": "n_si", "expression": 3.47, "description": "silicon refractive index"},
    {"name": "n_d2o", "expression": 1.32, "description": "D2O refractive index"},
    {"name": "lambda_t", "expression": "1496*nm", "description": "target wavelength (the measured cavity resonance)"},
    {"name": "k_fraction", "expression": 0.5, "description": "Bloch kz in units of 2 pi / a (0.5 = zone edge)"}
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": "w/2 + pad",
    "size_y": "thickness/2 + pad",
    "size_z": "a",
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
```

### `linear_taper_eme` - Linear taper (EME)

EME, stationary: a 10 um linear taper from a 0.5 um to a 2 um wide guide (eps 4 in silica, ez, 1.55 um, 25 nm mesh, cpml on y, pec x faces); eme_ports with the fundamental incident from x_lo, the section table, derived T1 = transmission into the fundamental of the wide guide, and eme_length_sweep: T1 versus the taper length from 2.5 to 40 um, re-cascaded from the one solve.

### `mmi_1x2_eme` - 1 x 2 MMI splitter (EME)

EME, stationary, ez at 1.55 um: a centre-fed 1 x 2 multimode interference splitter (eps 4 in silica, 25 nm mesh, cpml on y, pec x faces): a 1 um input guide, a 3 um wide MMI section of length L_mmi (parameter, 7.3 um = 3 L_pi / 8 for the two-fold symmetric image, L_pi = 4 n_r W_e^2 / (3 lambda) with the effective width W_e ~ 3.36 um) and two 1 um output guides centred at +-0.75 um. The output port modes are the supermodes of the guide pair, so T1 (symmetric supermode) is the power split equally into both outputs and the antisymmetric one stays ~0 by symmetry. Beat-length check: the section table lists n_eff_1 / n_eff_2 of the MMI section (its two lowest, even and odd modes), and L_pi = lambda / (2 (n_eff_1 - n_eff_2)) of those modes is exact for the EME, which propagates every section mode with its own beta; set L_mmi = 3 L_pi / 8 from it to tune the splitter.

```json

  "title": "1 x 2 MMI splitter (EME)",

    "angle_min": 0.0,

    {"name": "mmi", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 3e-06, "y0": 1.5e-06, "x1": "3e-06 + L_mmi", "y1": 4.5e-06}},

      "kind": "rectangle",
      "params": {"x0": "3e-06 + L_mmi", "y0": 3.2500000000000002e-06, "x1": "2 * 3e-06 + L_mmi", "y1": 4.25e-06}

    {
```

### `thermo_optic_phase_shifter_eme_3d` - Thermo-optic phase shifter (EME 3D)

Heat + em_eme_3d, stationary: a 0.5 x 0.2 um silicon strip in silica running 20 um along z, a 1.5 x 0.2 um heater region 1 um above it from z = 5 to 15 um dissipating P_heater (parameter, 1 mW), the substrate face y_lo at 293.15 K; steady heat, heat_em_thermo_optic, then the EME of the heated guide (pec transverse walls of the 4 x 3 um box, 50 nm cells, 10 modes, section_tolerance 1e-4: heated layers within about 1 K of a section's first layer merge into it, a staircase of a few percent of the phase shift). The transmission row of the eme_ports table gives the phase of S21 (sweep P_heater to get the phase shift, about 0.1 rad per mW here); derived T1 and the mean core temperature.

### `waveguide_thermal_noise` - Waveguide thermal noise

thermal_noise study of a 480 x 220 nm silicon strip in silica (library materials) at 1550 nm: em_modes on the half cross-section (x_lo antisymmetric mirror through the strip centre, quasi-TE family; em CPML on the other faces) and heat with the substrate sink T = 293.15 K on y_lo (2 um below the strip), other faces insulated. One thermorefractive_noise feature on all cells (mode_pair: the quasi-TE mode backscattered into itself, q0 = 2 beta, uniform over 1 mm): psd in 1/Hz (backscattered power per incident power per Hz) with the silica and silicon self terms and their cross term. 29 log-spaced frequencies 1000..1e+10 Hz, uniform 20 nm mesh; derived values n_eff_1 and the PSD at 1 MHz.

One thermorefractive_noise feature on all cells (mode_pair: the quasi-TE mode backscattered into itself, q0 = 2 beta, uniform over 1 mm): psd in 1/Hz (backscattered power per incident power per Hz) with the silica and silicon self terms and their cross term. 29 log-spaced frequencies 1000..1e+10 Hz, uniform 20 nm mesh; derived values n_eff_1 and the PSD at 1 MHz.",

### `flux_concentrator_needle` - Flux-concentrator needle

Magnetostatics, axisymmetric, stationary sweep: a Metglas needle (r = 31 um, l = 15 mm, mu_dc 1e5) in a uniform axial field B_ext = 1e-4 T concentrates the flux at its tip; derived B_sensor (mean |B| over a 31 um disc a gap below the tip) and gain = B_sensor / B_ext (EF = 20 log10(gain) dB), swept over gap = 25 .. 400 um.

### `iron_c_core_saturation` - Iron C-core saturation

Magnetostatics on a triangle mesh, planar, stationary sweep: an iron C-core (80 x 60 mm, 10 mm limbs, mu_dc 4000, saturation_polarization 1.8 T) with a gap_width = 2 mm air gap in its right limb, driven by a 200-turn coil around the left limb (go and return sides as two coil features); I_coil swept 0.5 .. 32 A. Derived B_gap = mean |B| in the gap against the ideal mu0 N I / g (analytic curve, valid below the knee: the core reluctance takes ~3 %); Newton (nonlinear_method auto) solves the saturation.

```json
{
  "title": "Iron C-core saturation",
  "description": "Magnetostatics on a triangle mesh, planar, stationary sweep: an iron C-core (80 x 60 mm, 10 mm limbs, mu_dc 4000, saturation_polarization 1.8 T) with a gap_width = 2 mm air gap in its right limb, driven by a 200-turn coil around the left limb (go and return sides as two coil features); I_coil swept 0.5 .. 32 A. Derived B_gap = mean |B| in the gap against the ideal mu0 N I / g (analytic curve, valid below the knee: the core reluctance takes ~3 %); Newton (nonlinear_method auto) solves the saturation.",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "I_coil", "expression": 1.0, "description": "coil current per turn [A]"},
    {"name": "N_turns", "expression": 200, "description": "turns of the coil"},
    {"name": "gap_width", "expression": 0.002, "description": "air gap height [m]"}
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 0.16,
    "size_y": 0.12,
    "size_z": null,
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"
  },
  "geometry": [
    {"name": "outer", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 0.04, "y0": 0.03, "x1": 0.12, "y1": 0.09}},
    {"name": "window", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 0.05, "y0": 0.04, "x1": 0.11, "y1": 0.08}},
    {
      "name": "gap",
      "kind": "rectangle",
      "params": {"x0": 0.11, "y0": "60e-3 - gap_width / 2", "x1": 0.12, "y1": "60e-3 + gap_width / 2"}
    },
    {"name": "core", "kind": "difference", "params": {"base": "outer", "subtract": ["window", "gap"]}},
    {
      "name": "gap_probe",
      "kind": "rectangle",
      "params": {"x0": 0.112, "y0": "60e-3 - gap_width / 4", "x1": 0.11800000000000001, "y1": "60e-3 + gap_width / 4"}
    },
    {
      "name": "coil_in",
      "kind": "rectangle",
      "params": {"x0": 0.051000000000000004, "y0": 0.045, "x1": 0.057, "y1": 0.075}
    },
    {"name": "coil_out", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": 0.033, "y0": 0.045, "x1": 0.039, "y1": 0.075}}
  ],
  "selections": [],
  "materials": [
    {
      "name": "iron",
```

### `flux_concentrator_needle_ac` - Flux-concentrator needle, AC

Magnetic fields AC, axisymmetric, stationary sweep over the frequency f = 10 Hz .. 30 kHz: the flux_concentrator_needle model (Metglas needle r = 31 um, l = 15 mm, mu_dc 1e5, applied axial field amplitude B_ext = 1e-4 T) with the needle conductivity sigma_needle = 7e5 S/m and magnetic loss mu_dc_loss = loss_tangent mu_dc: eddy currents screen the needle once the skin depth falls below its radius (~1 kHz), so the gain = B_sensor / B_ext (mean |B| over a 31 um disc a gap = 100 um below the tip; EF = 20 log10(gain) dB) drops with frequency.

```json
{
  "title": "Flux-concentrator needle, AC",
  "description": "Magnetic fields AC, axisymmetric, stationary sweep over the frequency f = 10 Hz .. 30 kHz: the flux_concentrator_needle model (Metglas needle r = 31 um, l = 15 mm, mu_dc 1e5, applied axial field amplitude B_ext = 1e-4 T) with the needle conductivity sigma_needle = 7e5 S/m and magnetic loss mu_dc_loss = loss_tangent mu_dc: eddy currents screen the needle once the skin depth falls below its radius (~1 kHz), so the gain = B_sensor / B_ext (mean |B| over a 31 um disc a gap = 100 um below the tip; EF = 20 log10(gain) dB) drops with frequency.",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "B_ext", "expression": 0.0001, "description": "applied axial flux density [T] (AC: amplitude)"},
    {"name": "mu_needle", "expression": 100000.0, "description": "relative permeability of the needle"},
    {"name": "gap", "expression": 0.0001, "description": "distance from the lower needle tip to the sensor centre [m]"},
    {"name": "f", "expression": 1000.0, "description": "frequency [Hz]"},
    {"name": "sigma_needle", "expression": 700000.0, "description": "needle conductivity [S/m]"},
    {
      "name": "loss_tangent",
      "expression": 0.01,
      "description": "magnetic loss tangent mu_dc_loss / mu_dc of the needle"
    }
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 0.01,

      "params": {"x0": 0.0, "y0": "0.0125 - gap - 5e-06", "x1": 3.1e-05, "y1": "0.0125 - gap + 5e-06"}
```

### `needle_3d` - Flux concentrator needle (3D)

Magnetostatics (3D, stationary): a 15 mm x 125 um x 25 um Metglas needle (mu_dc 1e5) along z in a uniform axial field B_ext (external_field, 10 x 10 x 35 mm window held at the applied potential), the geometry of Carey et al. 2026 on coarse 25 um cells around the needle and its lower tip (graded by 1.3 to 1 mm, by 1.5 to 2 mm outside). Derived values: gain_h50 = mean |B| / B_ext over a sensor box 50 um below the tip and EF_h50 = 20 log10(gain) against the prolate-spheroid estimate (Eq. 2 with m = l / sqrt(w t)); line curve B_axis: |B| on the needle axis below the tip against Eq. 2. The converged, paper-matched run is experiments/Ben_flux/needle_3d_scalar_potential.

```json
{
  "title": "Flux concentrator needle in 3D",
  "description": "",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "B_ext", "expression": 2.1e-05, "description": "applied axial flux density [T]"},
    {"name": "mu_needle", "expression": 100000.0, "description": "relative permeability of the needle"},
    {"name": "l_needle", "expression": "15*mm", "description": "needle length (z)"},
    {"name": "w_needle", "expression": "125*um", "description": "needle width (x)"},
    {"name": "t_needle", "expression": "25*um", "description": "needle thickness (y)"},
    {"name": "Lx", "expression": "10*mm", "description": "window size x"},
    {"name": "Ly", "expression": "10*mm", "description": "window size y"},
    {"name": "Lz", "expression": "35*mm", "description": "window size z (along the field)"},
    {"name": "x_c", "expression": "Lx/2", "description": "needle axis x"},
    {"name": "y_c", "expression": "Ly/2", "description": "needle axis y"},
    {"name": "z_tip", "expression": "10*mm", "description": "z of the lower needle tip"},
    {"name": "z_top", "expression": "z_tip + l_needle", "description": "z of the upper needle tip"},
    {"name": "h_sensor", "expression": "50*um", "description": "sensor height below the tip"},
    {
      "name": "m_aspect",
      "expression": "l_needle/sqrt(w_needle*t_needle)",
      "description": "needle aspect ratio m = l / sqrt(w t)"
    },
    {"name": "s_eq2", "expression": "l_needle/m_aspect", "description": "decay length of Eq. 2, sqrt(w t)"},
    {
      "name": "N_demag",
      "expression": "(m_aspect/sqrt(m_aspect**2 - 1)*log(m_aspect + sqrt(m_aspect**2 - 1)) - 1)/(m_aspect**2 - 1)",
      "description": "demagnetization factor of a prolate spheroid of aspect m"
    },
    {
      "name": "G_tip",
      "expression": "mu_needle/(N_demag*(mu_needle - 1) + 1)/m_aspect",
      "description": "Eq. 1-2 tip gain B0 / (m B_ext)"
    }
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": "Lx",
    "size_y": "Ly",
    "size_z": "Lz",
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"
  },
  "geometry": [
    {
      "name": "needle",
      "kind": "box",
      "params": {
        "x0": "x_c - w_needle/2",
        "y0": "y_c - t_needle/2",
        "z0": "z_tip",
        "x1": "x_c + w_needle/2",
        "y1": "y_c + t_needle/2",
        "z1": "z_top"
      }
    },
    {
      "name": "sensor",
      "kind": "box",
      "params": {
        "x0": "x_c - 68*um",
        "y0": "y_c - 43*um",
        "z0": "z_tip - h_sensor - 12.5*um",
        "x1": "x_c + 68*um",
        "y1": "y_c + 43*um",
        "z1": "z_tip - h_sensor + 12.5*um"
      }
    }
  ],
  "selections": [],
  "materials": [
    {"name": "air", "library": null, "properties": {"mu_dc": 1.0}, "dispersion": null, "selection": ["all"]},
    {
      "name": "metglas",
      "library": null,
      "properties": {"mu_dc": "mu_needle"},
      "dispersion": null,
      "selection": ["needle"]
    }
  ],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "magnetic",
      "type": "magnetostatics_3d",
      "settings": {},
      "features": [
        {
          "name": "applied",
          "type": "external_field",
          "selection": ["all"],
          "params": {"B_x": 0.0, "B_y": 0.0, "B_z": "B_ext"},
          "enabled": true
        }
      ],
      "every": 1,
      "solvers": {},
      "mesh": ""
    }
  ],
  "couplings": [],
  "meshes": [
    {
      "name": "mesh",
      "kind": "tensor",
      "mode": "nonuniform",
      "dx": "1*mm",
      "max_ratio": 1.3,
      "wavelength_min": null,
      "points_per_wavelength": null,
      "refinements": [],
      "outer_dx": "2*mm",
      "outer_ratio": 1.5,
      "axes": [
        {
          "axis": "x",
          "max_ratio": null,
          "bands": [{"lo": "x_c - 0.1*mm", "hi": "x_c + 0.1*mm", "dx": "25*um"}],
          "edges": [],
          "segments": []
        },
        {
          "axis": "y",
          "max_ratio": null,
          "bands": [{"lo": "y_c - 0.05*mm", "hi": "y_c + 0.05*mm", "dx": "25*um"}],
          "edges": [],
          "segments": []
        },
        {
          "axis": "z",
          "max_ratio": null,
          "bands": [
            {"lo": "z_tip - 0.3*mm", "hi": "z_tip + 50*um", "dx": "25*um"},
            {"lo": "z_tip", "hi": "z_top", "dx": "0.5*mm"},
            {"lo": "z_top - 50*um", "hi": "z_top + 50*um", "dx": "50*um"}
          ],
          "edges": [],
          "segments": []
        }
      ],
      "h_min": null,
      "curvature_factor": 0.3,
      "mesher": "builtin",
      "sweep_axis": "none",
      "pml_layers": 8,
      "mirror_x": false,
      "mirror_y": false,
      "size_points": [],
      "face_sizes": []
    }
  ],
  "solvers": [],
  "solver_defaults": {
    "backend": "auto",
    "precision": "auto",
    "subpixel_smoothing": false,
    "conformal_boundaries": false,
    "memory_check": "error"
  },
  "study": {
    "type": "stationary",
    "settings": {},
    "stored_fields": ["magnetic.B_abs", "magnetic.phi_m"],
    "sweep": null,
    "stored_cuts": [["y", "y_c"]],
    "adaptation": null,
    "estimation": null,
    "solved_physics": []
  },
  "derived_values": [
    {
      "name": "gain_h50",
      "expression": "magnetic_B_abs/B_ext",
      "selection": ["sensor"],
      "operation": "mean",
      "source": null,
      "at": null,
      "reference": null,
      "tolerance": null,
      "window": [],
      "wavelength": null,
      "fit": "",
      "degree": 1,
      "point": ""
    },
    {
      "name": "EF_h50",
      "expression": "20*log10(gain_h50)",
      "selection": [],
      "operation": "mean",
      "source": null,
      "at": null,
      "reference": "20*log10(G_tip + (1 - G_tip)*(h_sensor)/sqrt((h_sensor)**2 + s_eq2**2))",
      "tolerance": null,
      "window": [],
      "wavelength": null,
      "fit": "",
      "degree": 1,
      "point": ""
    }
  ],
  "analytic_curves": [
    {
      "name": "B_axis",
      "target": "line",
      "expression": "B_ext*(G_tip + (1 - G_tip)*(z_tip - z)/sqrt((z_tip - z)**2 + s_eq2**2))",
      "series": "",
      "line": ["x_c + 1*um", "y_c + 1*um", "z_tip - 0.3*mm", "x_c + 1*um", "y_c + 1*um", "z_tip - 12.5*um"],
      "field": "magnetic.B_abs",
      "x_column": "",
      "plane": null,
      "snapshot": null,
      "tolerance": null,
      "interface": "",
      "sampling": "nearest",
      "projection": ""
    }
  ],
  "derived_curves": []
}
```

### `solid_bloch_band_diagram_3d` - Phononic nanobeam band diagram (3D)

Solid mechanics 3D on builtin tetrahedra: one unit cell (a = 0.5 um) of a free-standing silicon nanobeam (0.25 x 0.25 um core) with a 0.75 um wide wing over the middle half of the period, periodic along x with bloch_kx = k_fraction 2 pi / a swept over 0-0.5 (Gamma-X); an eigenfrequency study per k lists the 8 bands nearest 4 GHz (resonance_table; target > 0 drops the rigid modes at Gamma). The bands per k form the band diagram; gaps between them are the phononic band gaps of the corrugation.

```json
{
  "title": "Phononic nanobeam band diagram (3D)",
  "description": "",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "a", "expression": 5e-07, "description": "lattice constant [m]"},
    {"name": "k_fraction", "expression": 0.5, "description": "Bloch kx in units of 2 pi / a (Gamma-X: 0-0.5)"}
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": 5e-07,
    "size_y": 7.5e-07,
    "size_z": 2.5e-07,
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"
  },
  "geometry": [
    {
      "name": "core",
      "kind": "box",
      "params": {
        "x0": 0.0,
        "y0": 2.5000000000000004e-07,
        "z0": 0.0,
        "x1": 5e-07,
        "y1": 5.000000000000001e-07,
        "z1": 2.5e-07
      }
    },
    {
      "name": "wing",
      "kind": "box",
      "params": {"x0": 1.25e-07, "y0": 0.0, "z0": 0.0, "x1": 3.75e-07, "y1": 7.5e-07, "z1": 2.5e-07}
    }
  ],
  "selections": [],
  "materials": [
    {
      "name": "silicon",
      "library": null,
      "properties": {"youngs_modulus": 169000000000.0, "poisson_ratio": 0.28, "rho": 2329.0},
      "dispersion": null,
      "selection": ["core", "wing"]
    }
  ],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "solid",
      "type": "solid_mechanics_3d",
      "settings": {
        "discretization": "quadratic",
        "periodic_x": true,
        "bloch_kx": "k_fraction * 2 * pi / a",
        "target_frequency": 4000000000.0
      },
      "features": [{"name": "bands", "type": "resonance_table", "selection": [], "params": {}, "enabled": true}],
      "every": 1,
      "solvers": {},
      "mesh": ""
    }
  ],
  "couplings": [],
  "meshes": [
```

### `heated_waveguide_stress` - Heated waveguide stress

Heat + solid mechanics (plane strain), stationary: cross-section of a 0.48 x 0.22 um silicon waveguide in silica under a 2 x 0.2 um gold heater (P_heater = 20 W/m), substrate y_lo held at 293.15 K and clamped, rollers on the sides; heat_solid_thermal_strain hands the temperature rise (~5 K at the core) to the solid; derived T_core, stress_xx_core, stress_yy_core, von_mises_max.

### `silicon_nitride_membrane_modes` - Silicon nitride membrane modes

A 1 x 0.7 mm silicon nitride membrane (50 nm film at 1 GPa stress: tension 50 N/m, areal density 1.55e-4 kg/m^2) clamped on its frame: the six lowest drum modes on quadratic triangles, f_mn = (c / 2) sqrt((m / a)^2 + (n / b)^2) with c = sqrt(T / rho_s) = 568 m/s (f_11 = 495 kHz), listed by the resonance table.

### `ppln_slab_shg` - PPLN slab waveguide SHG (QPM tuning curve)

Nonlinear FDFD (undepleted): a 1550 nm mode pumps a periodically poled z-cut lithium niobate slab in silica; sweep of the poling period over the QPM peak, forward second-harmonic mode power vs the coupled-mode sinc^2 curve.

```json
{
  "title": "PPLN slab waveguide SHG (QPM tuning curve)",
  "description": "Second-harmonic generation in a 0.6 um z-cut lithium niobate slab in silica (2D, E_z = the extraordinary index, d33): a 1550 nm fundamental-mode source pumps a 20 um periodically poled section; the nonlinear_frequency_domain study sweeps the poling period Lambda over the quasi-phase-matching peak, and the derived value P_sh (forward SH mode power, W/m) is drawn against the coupled-mode curve cmt_sinc2 = C_cmt d33^2 P_pump^2 L_pp^2 sinc^2(u_qpm). dbeta (the lattice-corrected mismatch of the continuum slab modes on the 15 nm mesh) and C_cmt (the mode overlap) hold for this slab, wavelength and mesh only: recompute them when changing those. LN extraordinary is a one-pole Lorentz fit to n_e at 1550 and 775 nm; silica is non-dispersive. Fields and powers are per unit length in z (W/m).",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "lambda_p", "expression": "1550*nm", "description": "pump vacuum wavelength"},
    {"name": "P_pump", "expression": 1000.0, "description": "pump mode power [W/m]"},
    {"name": "d33", "expression": 2.5e-11, "description": "LN d33 [m/V]"},
    {"name": "x_pp", "expression": 2e-06, "description": "start of the poled section [m]"},
    {"name": "L_pp", "expression": 2e-05, "description": "poled length [m]"},
    {"name": "Lambda", "expression": 5.059e-06, "description": "poling period [m] (swept)"},
    {
      "name": "dbeta",
      "expression": 1242075.3920388017,
      "description": "beta_2w - 2 beta_w of the slab modes on the mesh [rad/m]"
    },
    {
      "name": "C_cmt",
      "expression": 8.746875191738601e+20,
      "description": "coupled-mode prefactor of P_2w / (d33^2 P_w^2 L^2)"
    },
    {"name": "u_qpm", "expression": "(dbeta - 2*pi/Lambda)*L_pp/2", "description": "QPM detuning phase [rad]"}
  ],
  "domain": {
    "size_x": "x_pp + L_pp + 2*um",
    "size_y": 1.8e-06,
    "size_z": null,
    "coordinates": "cartesian",
    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"
  },
  "geometry": [
    {
      "name": "ln_slab",
      "kind": "rectangle",
      "params": {"x0": 0.0, "y0": 6e-07, "x1": "x_pp + L_pp + 2*um", "y1": 1.2e-06}
    },
    {"name": "poled", "kind": "rectangle", "params": {"x0": "x_pp", "y0": 6e-07, "x1": "x_pp + L_pp", "y1": 1.2e-06}},
    {"name": "source_line", "kind": "line", "params": {"x0": 1e-06, "y0": 0.0, "x1": 1e-06, "y1": 1.8e-06}},
    {
      "name": "monitor_line",
      "kind": "line",
      "params": {"x0": "x_pp + L_pp + 1*um", "y0": 0.0, "x1": "x_pp + L_pp + 1*um", "y1": 1.8e-06}
    }
  ],
  "selections": [],
  "materials": [
    {"name": "silica", "library": "silica", "properties": {}, "dispersion": null, "selection": ["all"]},
    {
      "name": "ln_extraordinary",
      "library": null,
      "properties": {"eps_r": 1.277394},
      "dispersion": [{"model": "lorentz", "delta_eps": 3.23696, "omega_0": 9418257836544266.0, "gamma": 0.0}],
      "selection": ["ln_slab"]
    }
  ],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "nl",
      "type": "em_fdfd_nonlinear",
      "settings": {},
      "features": [
        {
          "name": "p",
          "type": "harmonic_channel",
          "selection": [],
          "params": {"definition": "wavelength", "wavelength": "lambda_p", "polarization": "ez"},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "sh",
          "type": "harmonic_channel",
          "selection": [],
          "params": {"definition": "combination", "combination": "2 p", "polarization": "ez"},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "ppln",
          "type": "chi2_medium",
          "selection": ["poled"],
          "params": {
            "crystal_class": "3m",
            "d33": "d33",
            "orientation": "z_cut",
            "poling": "sign(sin(2*pi*(x - x_pp)/Lambda))"
          },
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "pump",
          "type": "mode_source",
          "selection": ["source_line"],
          "params": {"channel": "p", "mode_number": 1, "direction": "positive", "power": "P_pump", "phase": 0.0},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "pump_out",
          "type": "mode_monitor",
          "selection": ["monitor_line"],
          "params": {"channel": "p", "mode_number": 1},
          "enabled": true
        },
        {
          "name": "sh_out",
          "type": "mode_monitor",
          "selection": ["monitor_line"],
          "params": {"channel": "sh", "mode_number": 1},
          "enabled": true
        }
      ],
      "every": 1,
```

### `gaas_disk_shg` - GaAs disk SHG (mixed polarisation)

Nonlinear FDFD (undepleted): an H_z plane wave at 1550 nm on a GaAs disk generates an E_z second harmonic through d14; sweep of the crystal angle (90 degree period), SH far field.

```json
{
  "title": "GaAs disk SHG (mixed polarisation)",
  "description": "Second-harmonic generation in a GaAs disk with the [001] axis out of the plane (2D, -43m d14): an in-plane polarised (H_z) plane wave at 1550 nm drives P_z = 2 eps0 d14 E_x E_y in crystal axes, so the second harmonic is E_z polarised: the pump channel p is hz and the SH channel sh ez. The study sweeps the crystal angle theta_c (degrees, the rotation of the crystal axes about z): the generated power P_sh (power_exchange_sh, W/m) repeats every 90 degrees, and the far field of the SH changes with the angle. GaAs is a lossless one-pole Lorentz fit to n at 1550 and 775 nm (its absorption at 775 nm is not modelled).",

    {"name": "lambda_p", "expression": "1550*nm", "description": "pump vacuum wavelength"},
```

### `silicon_thg_waveguide` - Silicon slab waveguide THG (absorbing silicon)

Nonlinear FDFD (undepleted chi3): a guided pump mode in a silicon slab waveguide generates the third harmonic in dispersive, absorbing silicon; sweep of the pump wavelength, forward and backward third-harmonic power.

```json
{
  "title": "Silicon slab waveguide THG (absorbing silicon)",
  "description": "Third-harmonic generation in a 220 nm silicon slab waveguide in silica (2D, E_z): a guided pump mode drives a 4 um chi3 section (cubic silicon tensor, n2 at 1550 nm with the index of each cell); the third harmonic is a generated channel th = 3 p in the undepleted mode. Silicon is the library entry with a three-pole fit of its tabulated n, k (440-1450 nm), so the harmonic sees the strong dispersion and absorption of silicon below 500 nm: it is absorbed within about a micrometre and far from phase matched, and leaves mostly through the ends of the section. The study sweeps the pump wavelength; P_th_forward / P_th_backward are the TH fluxes beyond the section, P_th_generated the power the polarisation delivers (the rest is absorbed). One chi3 serves THG and SPM (thg_scale = 1); set thg_scale for a measured THG susceptibility. Fields and powers are per unit length in z (W/m).",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "lambda_p", "expression": "1400*nm", "description": "pump vacuum wavelength (swept)"},
    {"name": "P_pump", "expression": 1000000.0, "description": "pump mode power [W/m]"},
    {"name": "n2_si", "expression": 4.5e-18, "description": "silicon Kerr index at 1550 nm [m^2/W]"},
    {"name": "x_nl", "expression": 2e-06, "description": "start of the chi3 section [m]"},
    {"name": "L_nl", "expression": 4e-06, "description": "chi3 section length [m]"}

    },
    {
      "name": "nl_section",
      "kind": "rectangle",
      "params": {"x0": "x_nl", "y0": 8.9e-07, "x1": "x_nl + L_nl", "y1": 1.11e-06}
```

### `shg_saturation_tanh` - Pump-depleted SHG saturation (tanh^2)

Nonlinear FDFD (pump depleted, Newton-Krylov): phase-matched second-harmonic generation in a periodic strip with the pump amplitude swept into saturation; SH and depleted pump amplitudes against the coupled-mode tanh and sech curves.

```json
{
  "title": "Pump-depleted SHG saturation (tanh^2)",
  "description": "Second-harmonic generation into saturation (2D, coupling_mode pump_depleted, Newton-Krylov): a y-periodic strip (4 rows of 10 nm, CPML along x) filled with a birefringent medium whose E_z (pump, 1550 nm, eps_zz 2.25) and H_z (second harmonic, eps_yy set by the template) lattice waves are exactly phase matched, and a 20 um chi2 slab (general d23 = d34 = 25 pm/V). The study sweeps Gamma L, the pump amplitude being E0 = gamma_L / G_coef; A_sh (forward SH, |E_y| at the cell centre) and A_pump (transmitted pump |E_z|) are drawn against the lattice coupled-mode curves E0 kappa_ratio tanh(Gamma L) and E0 / cosh(Gamma L) (accuracy ~1 %: coupled-mode theory, backward waves). G_coef and kappa_ratio hold for this strip only. Fields are per unit length in z.",
```

### `silicon_fwm_waveguide` - Silicon waveguide FWM with TPA and free carriers

Nonlinear FDFD (pump depleted, Newton-Krylov): degenerate four-wave mixing in a silicon slab waveguide with two-photon and free-carrier absorption; sweep of the pump power, idler conversion and the nonlinear losses.

```json
{
  "title": "Silicon waveguide FWM with TPA and free carriers",
  "description": "Degenerate four-wave mixing in a 220 nm silicon slab waveguide in silica (2D, E_z, coupling_mode pump_depleted with the Newton-Krylov iteration): pump p (1550 nm) and signal s (1560 nm) mode sources, the idler i = 2 p - s (1540 nm) generated in a 40 um chi3 section (cubic silicon tensor, n2, two-photon absorption and steady-state free carriers with a 1 ns lifetime). The study sweeps the pump power: eta_idler = P_idler / P_signal grows as P_pump^2 at low power and saturates when two-photon and free-carrier absorption (TPA_loss, FCA_loss, peak carrier density N_peak) and the carrier index change set in. All mixing products off the three channels are dropped (validation info). Silicon is non-dispersive here (n 3.476): phase matching comes from the slab dispersion only. Fields and powers are per unit length in z (W/m): the 2D intensity is about the power over 0.3 um.",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "P_pump", "expression": 1000000.0, "description": "pump mode power [W/m] (swept)"},
    {"name": "P_signal", "expression": 10000.0, "description": "signal mode power [W/m]"},
    {"name": "n2_si", "expression": 4.5e-18, "description": "silicon Kerr index at 1550 nm [m^2/W]"},
    {"name": "beta_si", "expression": 8e-12, "description": "silicon two-photon absorption at 1550 nm [m/W]"},

    "radius_min": 0.0,
    "angle_min": 0.0,
    "angle_wrap": "none"

    {
```

### `gold_ridge_surface_shg` - Gold ridge surface SHG

Nonlinear FDFD (undepleted, surface chi2): an in-plane polarised 1550 nm plane wave on a gold ridge generates second harmonic at the metal surface; sweep of the ridge width, SH power and far field.

```json
{
  "title": "Gold ridge surface SHG",
  "description": "Surface second-harmonic generation of a gold ridge (2D, in vacuum): an in-plane polarised (H_z) plane wave at 1550 nm drives the isotropic surface susceptibility of the gold boundary (surface_chi2: chi_s_nnn, chi_s_ntt, chi_s_tnt, normal field taken inside the metal, sites on the cell faces, exact for this axis-aligned ridge whose swept edges lie on cell edges). At normal incidence a flat surface radiates no SH, so the harmonic comes from the corners and the faces normal to the incident field; P_sh (power_exchange_sh, W/m) follows the ridge width w_ridge (swept). Gold is the library Drude entry (fit 0.6-2 um); the surface coefficients are illustrative orders of magnitude, not fitted values.",
  "version": 2,
  "parameters": [
    {"name": "lambda_p", "expression": "1550*nm", "description": "pump vacuum wavelength"},
    {"name": "E0", "expression": 100000000.0, "description": "incident in-plane field [V/m]"},
    {"name": "w_ridge", "expression": "200*nm", "description": "ridge width (swept)"},
    {"name": "h_ridge", "expression": "100*nm", "description": "ridge height"},
    {"name": "chi_nnn", "expression": 1e-18, "description": "chi_s_nnn [m^2/V]"},
```

### `graphene_thg_grating` - Graphene on a nitride grating: THG

Nonlinear FDFD (undepleted, sigma3 sheet): third harmonic of graphene on a silicon nitride grating; pump wavelength sweep across the guided-mode resonance, TH power up and down.

```json
{
  "title": "Graphene on a nitride grating: THG",
  "description": "Third-harmonic generation of a graphene sheet on a silicon nitride grating (2D, E_z, x-periodic): the grating couples the pump into the guided mode of the nitride slab, so the field at the graphene and its third harmonic (sheet_nonlinearity sigma3, K = sigma3 E^3 / 4) peak at the guided-mode resonance. conducting_sheet (graphene Kubo, E_F 0.4 eV) adds the linear sheet response on the same line. The study sweeps the pump wavelength; P_th_up / P_th_down are the TH fluxes above and below (W/m per unit length in z, per period), T_pump the pump transmission. sigma3 is an illustrative magnitude (graphene THG values depend strongly on E_F and the photon energy).",

  "parameters": [
    {"name": "lambda_p", "expression": "1550*nm", "description": "pump vacuum wavelength (swept)"},

    {"name": "sigma3_thg", "expression": 1e-21, "description": "graphene THG sigma3 [S m^2/V^2]"},
    {
      "name": "P_inc",
      "expression": "E0**2 / (2 * 376.73031355893636) * 9e-07",
      "description": "incident pump power per period [W/m]"
    }

    {"name": "graphene_line", "kind": "line", "params": {"x0": 0.0, "y0": 1.555e-06, "x1": 9e-07, "y1": 1.555e-06}},

    {

    }
  ],
  "physics": [
    {
      "name": "nl",
      "type": "em_fdfd_nonlinear",

          "enabled": true

        {
```

### `shg_metasurface_optimization` - Inverse-designed SHG metasurface

em_fdfd_nonlinear (E_z channels p at 1.55 um and sh = 2 p, undepleted cascade), optimization study: a y-periodic 0.5 um cell with a 0.3 um z-cut lithium niobate film (d33 25 pm/V, eps 4.58) on silica, pumped at normal incidence by a 1e7 V/m line source in air. A 0.8 um design layer on the film (density from air to silicon nitride, eps 1 .. 4, filter radius 60 nm, tanh projection reaching beta 8 over 3 continuation stages, at most 30 L-BFGS-B iterations each) is shaped to maximise the second-harmonic flux transmitted into the silica (adjoint_objective of channel sh): the layer focuses and phase-matches the pump in the film. The design_density field is stored per iteration; the design_region result shows J and the grayness versus iteration. 2D: powers per unit length in z.


### `thermo_optic_heater` - Thermo-optic heater

Electric currents + heat + EM FDFD (E_z), stationary: a gold strip heater (1 x 8.8 um, V_heater = 0.02 V, sigma_dc(T)) 1.52 um beside a 0.48 um silicon slab waveguide in silica; Joule heating (~2 kW/m), out-of-plane sink (7e5 W/(m^2 K) through 1 um: heater rise ~60-80 K, waveguide ~20 K), thermo-optic phase of the guided mode (~0.1 rad, mode_monitor phase_positive); derived P_heater, T_max, T_waveguide.

### `silicon_pn_photodiode` - Silicon pin photodiode

drift_diffusion, stationary: a lateral p+ / i / n+ silicon diode (4 x 0.2 um, 10 nm cells) reverse-biased by V_reverse = 1 V, with a uniform generation G_light = 1e27 1/(m^3 s) in the 2 um intrinsic absorber (photocurrent ~ q G A = 6.4e-5 A/m, read from the contact tables); derived R_total.

### `induction_billet` - Induction heating of a billet

Magnetic fields AC + heat, axisymmetric, time-dependent: a 20-turn coil carrying I = 300 A peak at f = 1 kHz heats an aluminium billet (r = 20 mm, 60 mm tall) by eddy currents in its skin (delta ~2.7 mm); the eddy loss feeds transient heat for 20 s through magnetic_heat_induction. Probes record the surface and core temperature; derived the eddy loss P [W], the coil resistance and inductance and the hottest billet temperature.

Probes record the surface and core temperature; derived the eddy loss P [W], the coil resistance and inductance and the hottest billet temperature.",

### `yz_ln_saw_idt` - SAW from an IDT on YZ lithium niobate

Electrostatics + elastic_waves with electrostatics_acoustics_piezo (time_dependent): a 5-pair interdigital transducer (period 20 um, two driven terminals at +1 / -1 V, gaussian at f = v_R / period = 174 MHz, bandwidth 0.2) on a YZ lithium niobate substrate (a crystal entry, euler_deg 0, 90, 90: propagation along crystal Z, surface normal Y) launches surface acoustic waves; vy probes on the surface 100 and 200 um from the IDT. The literature Rayleigh velocity is ~3488 m/s (free surface); the coupling is two-way (bound charge: piezoelectric stiffening included, one electrostatics solve per acoustic step) and the in-plane c26 = c14 is stepped, so the remaining deviation is mesh dispersion (~20 cells per SAW wavelength) and the crystal data.

### `tfln_acousto_optic_modulator` - Thin-film lithium niobate acousto-optic modulator

Elastic_waves + electrostatics + EM FDFD (H_z), time_dependent, in the sagittal plane: a 3-pair interdigital transducer (period 4.4 um, terminals at +V_drive / -V_drive, cw at f = 3400 m/s / period = 773 MHz) on a 1 um Y-cut lithium niobate film (crystal entry, euler_deg 0, 90, 90: SAW along crystal Z, surface normal Y; eps_xx = n_e^2, eps_r = n_o^2) on silica launches a surface acoustic wave (two-way piezoelectric coupling) along the film, which also guides 1.55 um TM light along x: a mode_source (mode 1) left of the IDT, mode_monitors of modes 1 and 2 near the right end. acoustics_em_acousto_optic (rotated photoelastic tensor: the diagonal rows and the eps_xy row of the shear and normal strains) and electrostatics_em_pockels (clamped r of the IDT field and of the SAW piezoelectric field, r51 into eps_xy) feed the optics, which re-solves every 100 acoustic steps on the frozen state (stroboscopic snapshots). The period aims at TM0 -> TM1 phase matching, period = lambda / (n_eff,1 - n_eff,2), from slab estimates (n_eff ~2.08 / ~1.73); the template is not tuned by a run: read n_eff of both monitors and the SAW speed from the surface vy probe, then adjust the finger geometry and the frequency. Derived values: through_fraction and converted_fraction (mode 2) of the last solve. Not modelled: metal of the fingers in the optics (vacuum there), eps_xy in the port modes (they follow the strained diagonal eps of their line), acoustic and optical loss. Cost: ~6000 acoustic steps, each with an electrostatics solve, and ~60 sparse direct solves.

### `ln_phase_modulator` - Lithium niobate phase modulator

Electrostatics + EM FDFD (H_z) with the Pockels coupling, stationary, sweep over V_bias (0, 50, 100 V): an x-cut lithium niobate slab waveguide (1 um, along y, optic axis along x) in silica between two 10 um electrodes (terminals at +V_bias/2 and -V_bias/2, 3 um gap; pec_domain in the EM); derived phase (mode_monitor phase_positive) and E_x_core per value. Uniform-field estimate E = V / gap: V_pi L = lambda gap / (n_e^3 r33) = 1.5 V cm, 0.20 rad at 100 V; the silica gap (eps_dc 3.9 against 28 in the core) takes most of the voltage, so expect ~5x less (series estimate E = V / (w + (gap - w) 28 / 3.9)).
